{"forms":[{"form":"mass-energies","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Whereas in classical physics mass and energy are distinct concepts, on relativistic scales, they become indistinguishable and are replaced by mass-energy.","type":"example"},{"text":"In higher-energy physics, mass and energy eventually become meaningless as individual concepts; a particle's so-called \"mass\" is actually mass-energy and is often given equivalently in units of either mass (GeV/c²) or energy (GeV).","type":"example"},{"text":"For equations normalized with Planck units, the quantities of mass and energy become numerically identical, revealing their true nature as mass-energy.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Mass and energy as a unified concept."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["The energy associated with any given mass according to special relativity, E = mc²."],"qualifier":"also spelled mass energy","tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"upstream_line":3250663,"upstream_offset":8743638641,"upstream_record_sha256":"1c625d6728c76d63f41694555d06535403a0068b6f4ec34f5dac544c847b997a","word":"mass-energy"}

{"forms":[{"form":"switchbacks","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A railway track on a steep slope in a zigzag formation, in which a train travels in a reverse direction at each switch."],"tags":["attributive","figuratively","often"],"topics":["rail-transport","railways","transport"]},{"glosses":["A railway track on which there are steep ascents and descents, a train moving partially or fully by the force of gravity using the momentum generated when descending to travel up an ascending part of the track; especially (UK, dated), such a track built for fun rides at an amusement park; a type of rollercoaster."],"tags":["attributive","figuratively","often"],"topics":["rail-transport","railways","transport"]},{"glosses":["A flight path consisting of a series of steep ascents and descents, generally flown as a stunt."],"tags":["attributive","broadly","figuratively","often"],"topics":["aeronautics","aerospace","aviation","business","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A path or road having a series of steep ascents and descents."],"tags":["UK","attributive","broadly","figuratively","often"]},{"glosses":["A sharp bend in a path or road which causes a traveller to almost reverse their direction of travel, especially one of a series of such bends on an incline; a hairpin bend; also a path or road having such a series of bends."],"tags":["Canada","US","attributive","broadly","figuratively","often"],"topics":["road","transport"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈswɪt͡ʃbæk/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈswɪt͡ʃˌbæk/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":517520,"upstream_offset":2380545950,"upstream_record_sha256":"4a66031365483894b277c56e838c5ad3deac2a128a0f0a63730a8d9f41ea1fb0","word":"switchback"}

If so, will you do mine too, please?","type":"example"},{"text":"Won't you have another glass of wine? — No, I think I’ll go to bed.","type":"example"},{"text":"Can somebody lend me a hand? — I will.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm going to quit smoking. I really will!","type":"example"},{"text":"The President will arrive at 10.00 — Will she be wanting lunch?","type":"example"},{"text":"If you will come this way, I’ll show you your bedroom.","type":"example"},{"text":"I said I’d help, and help I will.","type":"example"},{"text":"If your sis won’t be here on Thu, we’d better cancel the booking. — I will pray that she arrives on time.","type":"example"},{"text":"That'll be £69.99, please. Last for ever this pair of jeans sure will.","type":"example"},{"text":"This breakthrough will mean that we spend less on the electricity bill.","type":"example"},{"text":"We'll finish ours sooner than you (do/will).","type":"example"},{"text":"Dad, will you take me to the park? Will you, please? — Will you be quiet! I'm on the phone.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'll hold that for you, shall I?","type":"example"},{"text":"If my plans change, I will let you know.","type":"example"},{"text":"If we don't go now, I will be late for my class.","type":"example"},{"text":"They will've forgotten what I look like by now.","type":"example"},{"text":"The baby will surely have green eyes, because both parents have.","type":"example"},{"text":"If you bring your tents, we will all camp on the coast.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to express the future tense, sometimes with an implication of volition or determination when used in the first person. Compare shall."],"tags":["auxiliary","no-past-participle"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Unfortunately, only one of these gloves will actually fit over my hand.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be able to, to have the capacity to."],"tags":["auxiliary","no-past-participle"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He will be home by now. He always gets home before 6 o'clock.","type":"example"},{"text":"I can't find my umbrella. I will've left it at home this morning.","type":"example"},{"text":"I’ll kill anybody who touches my car.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Expressing a present tense or perfect tense with some conditional or subjective weakening: \"will turn out to\", \"must by inference\"."],"tags":["auxiliary","no-past-participle"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Boys will be boys.","type":"example"},{"text":"People will talk.","type":"example"},{"text":"I will fall in love with the wrong women time and again.","type":"example"},{"text":"The shapes of clouds will often remind us of animals.","type":"example"},{"text":"Most nights I'll read a little before going to sleep.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To habitually do (a given action)."],"tags":["auxiliary","no-past-participle"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Will you marry me?","type":"example"},{"text":"What will you drink?","type":"example"},{"text":"I’ve told him three times, but he won’t take his medicine.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To choose or agree to (do something); used to express intention but without any temporal connotations, often in questions and negation."],"tags":["auxiliary","no-past-participle"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Do what you will.","type":"example"},{"text":"God willed it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To wish, desire (something)."],"tags":["literary","no-past-participle","transitive","uncommon"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Consider, if you will, the possibility that the sherry glasses were misplaced accidentally.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To wish or desire (that something happen); {"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"wills","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"wille","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Of course, man's will is often regulated by his reason.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One's independent faculty of choice; the ability to be able to exercise one's choice or intention."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Most creatures have a will to live.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of choosing to do something; a person’s conscious intent or volition."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Eventually I submitted to my parents' will.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One's intention or decision; someone's orders or commands."]},{"glosses":["Firmness of purpose, fixity of intent"],"synonyms":[{"word":"determination"},{"word":"firmness"},{"word":"resoluteness"},{"word":"resolve"}]},{"glosses":["A formal declaration of one's intent concerning the disposal of one's property and holdings after death; the legal document stating such wishes."],"synonyms":[{"word":"testament"},{"word":"last will"},{"word":"last will and testament"}],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["That which is desired; one's wish."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He felt a great will to make a pilgrimage to the Holy Land.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Desire, longing.
